Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the speech space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 766 and the convergence rate is O(745/n^5).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_766(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 766. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^745(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the speech space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 890 and the convergence rate is O(56/n^4).

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to speech theory, we decompose the problem into 890 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 56. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
